We propose GaLe, a memory-efficient technique that enables the deployment of pretrained networks on constrained devices without retraining. GaLe partitions feature maps into two components: a local exact (Le) representation that preserves fine details and a global approximate (Ga) representation that retains long-range dependencies. Unlike standard tiling, GaLe supports global operations and attention mechanisms found in hybrid CNN-transformer models. Validated on ImageNet, our method matches exact-inference performance while achieving up to 65% speedup and 90% RAM reduction on a Cortex-M33 compared to patch-based inference. We further demonstrate GaLe's versatility across classification, detection, and generation tasks, highlighting its potential as a foundation for resource-efficient architecture design.

</details>


### [102] [Generating Medical Image Counterfactuals using Causal Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02697)
*David A. Kelly,Tom Yaacov,Nathan Blake,Sander Beckers,Hana Chockler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需生成模型的可解释性医学图像反事实生成框架，直接从分类器自身的因果证据构建反事实图像，具有确定性、无需额外训练、支持区域可控编辑等优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实图像生成依赖GAN或扩散模型等辅助生成器，用黑盒解释黑盒，难以将分类器的决策过程与生成器的归纳偏置分离，限制了对模型决策边界的可信审计。

Method: 从分类器内部直接提取因果证据来构造反事实图像，无需任何生成模型。框架是确定性的，不要求额外训练，并支持在用户指定的感兴趣区域内进行可控编辑。

Result: 在真实医学影像数据集上的实验表明，该方法成功改变分类器预测，同时比生成式基线更贴近原始图像，提供了对分类器决策边界更直接透明的视图。

Conclusion: 该框架为医学影像分类器的可解释性审计提供了一个更直接、透明且无需生成模型的反事实解释方案。

Abstract: Deep learning models have achieved impressive performance in medical image diagnosis, yet their deployment in clinical settings remains constrained by limited explainability. Counterfactual images provide one means of auditing model behavior by showing how an image would need to change for a classifier to produce a different prediction. Existing approaches typically generate such explanations using auxiliary models, including generative adversarial networks and diffusion models. While often capable of producing visually realistic images, these methods explain one black-box model using another, making it difficult to separate the classifier's decision-making process from the inductive biases of the generator.
  We propose a novel counterfactual-generation framework that requires no generative model. Instead, counterfactuals are constructed directly from causal evidence extracted from the classifier. The resulting approach is deterministic, requires no additional model training, and enables controllable edits within user-specified regions of interest. Experiments on real-world medical imaging datasets demonstrate that the proposed method successfully changes classifier predictions while remaining closer to the original image than generative baselines, providing a more direct and transparent view of the classifier's decision boundary.

</details>


### [103] [A Top-Down Framework for Metric-Scale Athlete Localization from Single Broadcast Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02705)
*Thanh-Khoi Nguyen,Hoang-Phuc Nguyen,Linh-Huynh,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自顶向下的框架，从单帧广播画面中以米制精度定位运动员。核心创新包括边界感知自适应分块（解决超高清图像中极端尺度差异导致的目标截断问题）和将RTMPose-X改造为双关键点（骨盆与地面投影）估计器，通过相机标定射线投影确定性提升至世界坐标。在公开测试集上达到LocSim 97.44和mAP 0.9128，较基线提升超21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单帧广播画面中运动员的世界坐标定位极具挑战性，因为超高分辨率图像中存在极端尺度差异，导致标准滑窗推理出现目标被边界截断（recall下降）、尺度方差大等核心问题。

Method: （1）边界感知自适应分块（Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling）：基于粗粒度目标框预测迭代扩展分块边界，确保目标完整包含，轻量级管线适配无需改动网络架构；（2）将RTMPose-X适配为专用双关键点估计器（骨盆和地面投影点），使用重构的Gated Attention Unit处理这一几何耦合点对；（3）通过相机标定射线投影将2D地面投影点确定性提升至米制世界坐标。

Result: 在公开测试集上取得LocSim 97.44和mAP 0.9128，较基线提升超过21%。

Conclusion: 通过边界感知自适应分块有效缓解了极端尺度差异下的召回退化，将透视畸变隔离为残留定位误差的主要来源，建立了针对高分辨率尺度方差场景的鲁棒解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate world-coordinate localization of athletes from single-frame broadcast footage is inherently challenging due to extreme scale disparities in ultra-high-resolution imagery. In this paper, we propose a top-down framework for metric-scale athlete localization from a single calibrated frame. Our approach centers on three key contributions. First, we propose Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling, a semantics-guided extension of standard sliced inference. By iteratively expanding tile boundaries based on coarse bounding-box predictions, it systematically ensures full object containment, effectively mitigating boundary-splitting artifacts through a lightweight pipeline adaptation without architectural modifications. By substantially mitigating recall degradation under extreme scale variance, Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling enables us to isolate perspective distortion as the primary source of residual localization error. Second, we adapt the RTMPose-X architecture into a specialized two-keypoint estimator (pelvis and ground projection), employing a reformulated Gated Attention Unit optimized for this geometrically coupled point pair, and then deterministically lift the 2D ground projections into world coordinates via camera-calibrated ray casting. On the public test set, our method achieves a LocSim score of 97.44 and an mAP of 0.9128, outperforming the baseline by over 21 \% and establishing a robust solution for high-resolution scale variance.

</details>


### [104] [RVSD: Retrieval Vision Sparse Decoding for Mitigating Visual Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02731)
*Canjie Liu,Jiawen Kang,Jinbo Wen,Zishao Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RVSD提出了一种无需训练、即插即用的解码框架，首次在单次解码过程中统一了token稀疏化与语义空间视觉检索（SSVR），用于缓解大视觉语言模型中的视觉幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型在视觉语言任务中表现出色但仍容易出现视觉幻觉，现有缓解方法需要精选数据集、额外训练或多轮解码，计算开销大。

Method: RVSD包含两个核心组件：(1) 语义导向的token选择策略，选择性稀疏化冗余token同时保留关键视觉信息；(2) SSVR机制，将视觉补偿重构为共享语义空间中的按需跨模态检索过程。两者在同一解码过程中统一实现，无需训练。

Result: 大量实验表明RVSD在缓解视觉幻觉方面达到最先进性能，并在长上下文生成场景下保持稳健的幻觉抑制能力。

Conclusion: RVSD证明了一种无需额外训练和训练数据、计算开销低的解码级视觉幻觉抑制方法是可行且有效的，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved remarkable success in vision-language tasks. However, they remain prone to Visual Hallucinations (VHs), undermining their reliability in real-world applications. Existing solutions typically require curated datasets, additional training, or multi-round decoding, resulting in considerable computational overhead. In this paper, we propose \textbf{RVSD} (\underline{R}etrieval \underline{V}ision \underline{S}parse \underline{D}ecoding), a training-free and plug-and-play decoding framework that, for the first time, unifies token sparsification and \textbf{Semantic-Space Visual Retrieval} (SSVR) within a single decoding pass. Within RVSD, we introduce a \textbf{semantics-directed token selection} strategy that selectively sparsifies redundant tokens while preserving critical visual information. We further propose the SSVR mechanism, which reformulates visual compensation as an on-demand cross-modal retrieval process within a shared semantic space. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RVSD achieves state-of-the-art performance in mitigating VHs while maintaining robust suppression capabilities under long-context generation settings. Our code is available here.\footnote{https://github.com/canjie-liu/RVSD}

</details>


### [105] [InceptionGS: Generative Bootstrapping for Large-Scale Gaussian Splatting under Unstructured View Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02747)
*Tianheng Lu,Guangyu Wang,Ruqi Huang,Lu Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InceptionGS提出一种融合重建与生成的高斯泼溅方法，用于处理大规模场景中因视角稀疏导致的不完整重建问题。它从初始Gaussian Splatting出发，通过场景与视角自适应的生成先验，软性地修复观测不足的区域，同时保持其他区域的渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模场景数字化需要全视角一致且高质量的渲染，但采集覆盖所有细节的多视角图像成本过高且不可行，导致采样视角高度非结构化——大部分场景覆盖良好而某些区域必然缺乏观测。现有基于重建的方法在视角稀缺时脆弱，基于生成的方法则在泛化性、可控性和3D一致性上存在问题。

Method: 从初始Gaussian Splatting出发，通过软性地引入场景自适应与视角自适应的生成先验（generative priors），在修复因视角稀缺导致的问题区域的同时保持其他区域质量。其核心在于在重建与生成之间取得巧妙平衡，对哪些区域需要重建、哪些需要生成进行合理的区分与修补。

Result: 在大规模真实场景上的广泛实验证明了该方法在处理非结构化图像和提升高保真Gaussian Splatting方面的优越性与广泛适用性，并附有补充视频用于可视化演示。

Conclusion: InceptionGS通过重建与生成的平衡策略，有效解决了大规模场景重建中视角稀缺导致的区域退化问题，为复杂场景的高保真重建提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Achieving truly immersive large-scale scene digitization necessitates consistent and visually pleasing rendering across all possible viewing perspectives. However, collecting multi-view images covering every fine detail of a large-scale scene is prohibitive due to scene complexity, capture cost, negligence, or accessibility constraints. As a result, the sampled views tend to be highly unstructured -- the majority of the scene is well covered yet certain regions inevitably lack sufficient observations. Existing reconstruction based methods are vulnerable to view scarcity while generation based approaches suffer from generalization, controllability, and 3D consistency issues. To address this challenge, we propose InceptionGS, which bootstraps Gaussian splatting by subtly balancing reconstruction and generation. Starting from an initial Gaussian splatting, InceptionGS reasonably rethinks and repairs problematic regions caused by view scarcity while preserving the quality elsewhere, by softly incorporating scene- and view-adaptive generative priors. Extensive experiments on real-world large-scale scenes demonstrate the superiority and broad applicability of our approach in handling unstructured imagery and boosting high-fidelity Gaussian splatting. Please refer to the supplementary video for better visual demonstrations.

</details>


### [106] [Balancing Frequencies and Pixels in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02748)
*Lucas Degeorge,Paul Couairon,Arijit Ghosh,Alexei A. Efros,David Picard,Vicky Kalogeiton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Focal Log-Frequency Loss（f-loss），一种频谱平衡的损失函数，用于解决像素空间流模型中低频主导训练信号、高频细节学习缓慢的问题。通过先强调频域学习再过渡到像素空间v-loss的训练策略，无需架构改动即可作为流匹配损失的即插即用替代品，在多尺度模型上加速收敛最高40%并改善FID和感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像遵循1/f²频谱分布，低频包含大部分能量而重要的纹理和边缘只在稀疏的高频频带中。像素空间重建目标对所有空间误差一视同仁，导致低频主导优化信号，推迟了细粒度细节的学习。这种目标层面的频谱不平衡是训练像素空间流模型的关键低效环节。

Method: 提出f-loss（Focal Log-Frequency Loss），一种频谱平衡目标，在不同频率间均衡学习信号，特别强调像素空间目标中未被充分表示的高频成分。在此基础上提出两阶段训练策略：早期侧重频域学习以捕获所有频率，随后过渡到标准像素空间v-loss进行空间细化。该方法无需架构修改，可直接替换流匹配损失。

Result: 在多个模型规模下，该方法将收敛速度提升高达40%，同时一致性地改善了FID和感知保真度指标。

Conclusion: 通过频谱平衡的目标和两阶段训练策略，有效缓解了像素损失的低频偏差，使训练信号与模型不断演化的需求对齐，是一种概念简单且有效的流模型训练改进方案。

Abstract: Natural images follow a $1/f^2$ spectral distribution: most signal energy lies in the low spatial frequencies, while the perceptually important structures such as textures and edges occupy sparse high-frequency bands. Pixel-space reconstruction objectives, however, treat all spatial errors uniformly, causing low frequencies to dominate the optimization signal and delaying the learning of fine-scale details. In this work, we identify this objective-level spectral imbalance as a key inefficiency in training pixel-space flow models. To address it, we propose a Focal Log-Frequency Loss (f-loss), a spectrally balanced objective that equalizes the learning signal across frequencies, emphasizing high-frequency components that are otherwise underrepresented in pixel-space objectives. Building on this, we introduce a simple training strategy that combines frequency and pixel supervision: we first emphasize frequency-domain learning early to capture all frequencies, and then transition to standard pixel-space v-loss for spatial refinement. This balancing mitigates the low-frequency bias of pixel losses and aligns the training signal with the evolving needs of the model. Our approach is conceptually simple, requires no architectural changes, and acts as a drop-in replacement for flow matching losses. Across multiple model scales, it accelerates convergence by up to 40% while consistently improving FID and perceptual fidelity. We will release code and models.

</details>


### [107] [Multi-Tool Image Editing Attribution in Facial Forgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02751)
*Sheng Liu,Qiang Sheng,Danding Wang,Yu Li,Chenming Zhou,Juan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多工具图像编辑归属（MIEA）任务，用于识别一张人脸图像中涉及到的多个编辑工具。构建了包含50万+编辑人脸图像的MultiEdit数据集，覆盖换脸（Deepfake）和多种面部增强共六类编辑工具。设计了DPEC方法，结合空间与频域信息捕捉可区分、局部感知的工具痕迹，并采用基于错误的课程学习策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑归属方法基于单一工具假设，只能归因单个编辑工具，无法处理现实生活中越来越常见的多工具叠加编辑场景（多种编辑工具留下的痕迹是复合且重叠的）。

Method: 构建MultiEdit数据集（50万+编辑人脸图像，6种编辑工具，最多5步编辑操作）；设计DPEC（multi-tool attribution方法），从空间域和频域捕获可区分、局部感知的编辑工具痕迹，配合基于错误的课程学习策略进行训练。

Result: 在最多五步编辑的人脸图像上，DPEC优于九种对比方法。

Conclusion: 首次探索多工具图像编辑归属任务，证明了结合空间-频域信息与课程学习能有效识别叠加的多工具编辑痕迹，为真实复杂编辑场景下的取证与溯源提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As generative AI tools become increasingly powerful and easy to use, people can easily edit portrait images with a prompt, necessitating the task of image editing attribution, which predicts the involved editing tools from the given image. Existing attribution methods hold the single-tool assumption and can only attribute a specific editing tool, but struggle to handle the more complex and increasingly common multi-tool editing scenarios, where artifacts left by different editing tools are composite and overlapped. To address this gap, we explore Multi-Tool Image Editing Attribution (MIEA), which aims to identify multiple editing tools involved in a multi-tool edited facial image. To simulate the real-life editing operations on facial images, we then construct a new dataset, MultiEdit, which contains 500k+ edited facial images and covers six types of editing tools that support face swapping (Deepfake) and various facial enhancements. Inspired by the findings from data analysis, we design DPEC, a multi-tool attribution method that can capture distinguishable, locality-aware editing tool traces from both spatial and frequency domains with the support of an error-based curriculum learning strategy. Experiments show \Method\ outperforms nine methods for facial images edited in at most five steps.

</details>


### [108] [Video-Based Palm-Vein Authentication under Challenging Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02776)
*Xiaofeng Yan,Kechen Liu,Abhilash Venkatesh,Cathy Zhang,Xia Zhou,Salvatore Stolfo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍首个公开的视频级手掌静脉数据集CUP，涵盖清洁、温热、潮湿和脏污四种表面条件，并配套生理/人口统计元数据。作者在21种识别器上基准测试，发现脏污表面导致EER约翻4倍；通过时间维度的帧间共识和空间维度的无学习参数的显著性引导区域级最优传输匹配，在不重训的前提下将冻结SOTA骨干的平均EER降低29-37%，并以4.3M参数和3.1 GFLOPs全面领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手掌静脉生物识别在实际部署中会受到表面噪声（汗渍、污垢）、光照/运动变化及温度引起的血管可见度变化影响，但缺乏在这些条件下采集的数据来研究这些真实世界退化因素。

Method: 构建CUP视频数据集（4种表面条件+元数据）；测试21种静态/视频/多帧聚合架构；提出结合时间维度帧间共识（消除瞬态干扰）与空间维度显著性引导的区域级最优传输匹配（绕过脏污区域）的无参数测试时融合方法。

Result: 脏污表面使识别EER约翻4倍；提出方法在所有CUP表面于EER、TAR@FAR=0.01和Rank-1上最优，仅4.3M参数/3.1 GFLOPs；附着于4个冻结SOTA骨干平均EER降低29-37%；区域匹配在4个公开单图数据集上仍有增益；审计发现两个温热条件下（身体水分和性别）的显著差异。

Conclusion: 通过时间与空间双轴鲁棒性设计可大幅恢复脏污条件下的识别性能，且无需重训、无需额外学习参数，成本仅为视频模型的一小部分，证明该鲁棒性方案具有高实用性和通用性。

Abstract: Palm-vein biometrics are increasingly used for secure, contactless authentication. Yet real-world deployment exposes them to surface noise (sweat, dirt), illumination and motion variation, and temperature-driven changes in vascular visibility, which remain underexplored for lack of data captured under such conditions. To study these effects, we introduce the Columbia University Palm-vein (CUP) dataset, to our knowledge the first public video-based palm-vein dataset. CUP records every palm under four surface conditions (a clean baseline, warm, wet, and dirty) and pairs each subject with physiological and demographic metadata. On it we benchmark twenty-one recognizers spanning static, video, and multi-frame aggregation architectures. Models that verify reliably on clean palms lose most of their accuracy on dirty ones, and the mean equal error rate (EER) roughly quadruples. We recover much of that robustness along both axes of the capture. Temporally, a consensus over the few frames the sensor already returns cancels transient corruption; spatially, a test-time matcher that adds no learned parameters fuses the global cosine with a saliency-steered region-level optimal transport that routes the comparison around corrupted regions. The full design leads on every surface of CUP in EER, TAR@FAR=0.01, and Rank-1, at 4.3M parameters and 3.1 GFLOPs, a fraction of the video models' cost. Attached to four frozen state-of-the-art backbones it cuts their mean EER by 29-37% without retraining, and on four public single-image datasets the regional matching alone still helps. A preliminary audit across ten demographic and physiological traits finds two warm-condition gaps, along body water and gender, that survive multiple-comparison correction. CUP will be released for non-commercial research use at https://github.com/MobileX-CU/CUP_v1 upon publication.

</details>


### [109] [ShallowStream: Index Shallow then Answer Deep for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02780)
*Jitai Hao,Ke Yang,Qiang Huang,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShallowStream提出一种利用MLLM浅层同时进行帧编码与检索索引构建的流式视频理解框架，避免对每个新帧重复执行全深度prefill，将逐帧prefill延迟和10秒端到端延迟分别降低52.1x和11.9x，性能与最强现有流式方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解（具身智能、自动驾驶、监控等场景）中，持续视频流输入MLLM计算开销巨大。现有方法多聚焦token剪枝、合并、量化、按需取帧、上下文卸载等维度，忽视了模型深度这一维度——对每个到达帧重复执行全深度prefill导致KV cache随prefill深度线性增长，计算开销极高。

Method: ShallowStream利用MLLM的浅层层同时执行帧编码与检索索引构建：在流处理阶段，仅用浅层KV cache维护常开轻量索引；在查询应答阶段，利用浅层产生的注意力分数为上下文帧打分，并通过多样性感知的选择策略检索精确且全面的证据帧。

Result: 在流式视频理解任务上取得与最强现有流式方法相当的性能，同时将逐帧prefill延迟降低最高52.1x，10秒端到端延迟降低最高11.9x。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过利用浅层网络的编码能力替代全深度prefill，ShallowStream在保持流式视频理解精度的同时大幅降低了计算与显存开销，为MLLM流式推理提供了一条新的高效路径。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding is a critical capability for real-world applications, including embodied intelligence, autonomous driving, industrial monitoring, surveillance and early warning, and wearable assistants. However, processing continuous video streams with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is computationally expensive. Existing efforts have explored reducing streaming overhead through visual token pruning, token merging, quantization, on-demand frame retrieval, and context offloading. However, most existing methods overlook the dimension of model depth. Repeatedly executing full-depth MLLM prefill over incoming frames is prohibitively expensive, incurring substantial computational overhead and causing the KV cache to grow at a rate directly proportional to the prefill depth. To address these challenges, we propose ShallowStream, a novel framework that leverages the shallow layers of an MLLM to simultaneously perform frame encoding and retrieval index building. During stream processing, ShallowStream maintains an always-on lightweight index using the KV cache of shallow layers. During query-time answering, we leverage the attention scores generated by the shallow layers to score context frames and employ a diversity-aware selection strategy to retrieve precise and comprehensive evidence. ShallowStream achieves performance on par with the strongest existing streaming methods, while reducing per-frame prefill latency and 10-second end-to-end latency by up to 52.1x and 11.9x, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/CURRENTF/ShallowStream.

</details>


### [110] [AutoCompass: Accurate Visual Localization on Public Maps by Learning from Weak Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02798)
*Javier Tirado-Garín,Alan Savio Paul,Shuai Chen,Axel Barroso-Laguna,Tommaso Cavallari,Daniyar Turmukhambetov,Victor Adrian Prisacariu,Eric Brachmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoCompass是一种针对神经地图匹配器（neural map matchers）的监督训练方法，能够在绝对位姿标签存在噪声的情况下进行训练。核心发现包括：1) 标定（heading）标签并非必需，模型可从原始GPS标签自动学会预测精确航向；2) 在原始GPS标签周围定义容差区域可提升位置精度；3) 若可用，利用SLAM或SfM获取的训练图像间相对位姿可提供更精确的训练信号。在驾驶和以自我为中心的基准上均优于传统依赖绝对位姿标签的训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经地图匹配器依赖大规模地理参考图像数据集训练，但其中的位置和航向标签常含噪声，严重影响训练模型的精度。现有方法过度依赖绝对位姿标签的准确性，需要一种对标签噪声鲁棒的监督策略。

Method: 提出AutoCompass监督框架：1) 证明可去除heading标签，仅用GPS位置标签训练，模型自动学习航向预测；2) 在原始GPS位置周围定义软性的容差（tolerance）区域作为监督信号，以容忍位置噪声；3) 利用SLAM或SfM估计的训练图像间相对位姿作为补充的精确监督信号。

Result: 在驾驶场景和以自我为中心（egocentric）的多个基准数据集上，AutoCompass训练出的地图匹配模型在位置和航向精度上一致优于使用传统绝对位姿标签监督训练的同款模型。

Conclusion: 文章表明神经地图匹配器的训练无需强依赖绝对位姿标签（尤其是航向），通过对GPS噪声的容差建模和利用相对位姿作为补充信号，可以显著提升模型的位姿估计鲁棒性和精度。

Abstract: Neural map matchers estimate an image's 3-DoF pose relative to a 2D map. These models are trained on large-scale datasets of geo-referenced images, whose position and heading labels often contain noise that affects the trained models. To address this, we present AutoCompass, a supervision approach for training neural map matchers from inaccurate absolute pose labels. First, we show that heading labels are unnecessary: trained from raw GPS labels, models learn to predict accurate headings, automatically. Second, defining a tolerance region around raw GPS improves positional accuracy. Third, if available, our supervision uses relative poses between training images, obtained via SLAM or SfM, which provide a more accurate training signal. Across driving and egocentric benchmarks, AutoCompass consistently outperforms counterparts trained with the usual strong reliance on absolute pose labels.

</details>


### [111] [GDB-Reward: From Evaluation Metrics to Training Rewards for Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02813)
*Adrienne Deganutti,Purvanshi Mehta,Simon Hadfield,Andrew Gilbert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GDB-Reward框架，将异构的图形设计评估指标系统地转化为统一的强化学习奖励，用于优化冻结的文本到图像扩散模型，提升其在排版、布局、颜色等设计规范上的遵循度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像模型在自然图像合成上表现出色，但在图形设计任务中需要满足精确的排版、布局、颜色和视觉传达约束。微调扩散模型成本高昂，而提示优化需要信息丰富的奖励函数来训练冻结的生成器。现有的设计评估指标本可以充当奖励信号，但缺乏系统性框架将其转化为RL可用的奖励。

Method: 提出GDB-Reward框架，将异构的图形设计评估指标统一转化为单一的强化学习奖励。由于生成过程完全不可微，采用RL进行优化——RL不需要可微目标，只需要能对候选输出进行排序的标量奖励。框架系统化地将多种设计评估指标（涵盖感知质量、渲染保真度、空间准确性等）整合为RL奖励函数，在保持图像生成器完全冻结的情况下优化提示。

Result: 实验表明GDB-Reward提供了有效的优化目标，在感知质量、渲染保真度和空间精度方面显著提升了模型对设计规范的遵循度，且图像生成器保持完全冻结状态。

Conclusion: 异构的、不可微的评估指标可以超越被动基准测试的角色，在缺乏可微监督的领域中成为RL的有效优化目标。这为利用现有评估指标作为RL奖励来优化冻结生成模型提供了通用范式。

Abstract: Text-to-image models excel at natural image synthesis but struggle with graphic design, where success depends on satisfying precise constraints on typography, layout, color, and visual communication. While prompt optimization offers an attractive alternative to expensive diffusion model fine-tuning, learning prompts for frozen image generators requires informative reward functions despite the entirely non-differentiable generation process. Reinforcement learning does not require differentiable objectives; it requires only scalar rewards capable of ranking candidate outputs. This raises a simple question: can design evaluation metrics themselves become reinforcement learning rewards? Our central contribution is GDB-Reward, a framework that systematically transforms heterogeneous graphic design evaluation metrics into a unified reinforcement learning reward. Experiments demonstrate that GDB-Reward provides an effective optimization objective, substantially improving adherence to the design specification in perceptual quality, rendering fidelity, and spatial accuracy while keeping the image generator entirely frozen. More broadly, our results demonstrate that heterogeneous, non-differentiable evaluation metrics can move beyond passive benchmarking to become effective optimization objectives for reinforcement learning in domains where differentiable supervision is unavailable.

</details>


### [112] [Benchmarking RAW and RGB Restoration in Image Signal Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02831)
*Zihao Lu,Radu Timofte,Marcos V. Conde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统性地基准测试了在固定ISP（图像信号处理器）周围两种盲复原放置策略：RAW域（pre-ISP）复原与sRGB域（post-ISP）复原，覆盖四组智能手机、两种学习型ISP、三种退化模型（噪声、模糊、联合噪声与模糊）及多种代表性RAW和RGB复原模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代相机通过ISP将RAW传感器数据转换为sRGB图像，但盲图像复原任务中复原模块应放置在ISP之前（RAW域）还是之后（sRGB域）缺乏系统性对比研究，现有结论不一致且缺少统一基准。

Method: 构建统一基准AISP，在固定ISP条件下对比两种放置策略：pre-ISP RAW域复原与post-ISP sRGB域复原。涵盖四组智能手机设备、两种学习型ISP、三种退化场景以及若干代表性RAW和RGB复原模型，并引入ISP感知的RGB复原训练策略。

Result: 放置策略本身不决定性能。RAW域复原优于最佳通用RGB复原模型，但采用考虑ISP变换的ISP感知训练后的RGB复原模型取得了最佳整体性能。复原性能强烈依赖于复原模型与目标成像流水线之间的对齐程度。

Conclusion: 建议在研究报告中明确标注复原放置位置及是否使用ISP感知监督作为关键实验因素。代码已开源。

Abstract: Modern cameras transform RAW sensor measurements into sRGB images through an image signal processor (ISP). We benchmark two placements for blind restoration around a fixed ISP: (A) pre-ISP restoration in the RAW domain and (B) post-ISP restoration in the sRGB domain. The benchmark covers four smartphone device groups, two learned ISPs, three degradation regimes--noise, blur, and joint noise and blur--, and several representative RAW and RGB restoration models. Our results show that placement alone does not determine performance. The RAW restoration strategy outperforms the best generic RGB restoration models. However, RGB restoration models trained considering the ISP transformations, achieve the best overall performance. Our novel benchmark demonstrates that the image reconstruction performance strongly depends on the alignment between the restoration model and the target imaging pipeline. We consequently recommend reporting restoration placement and ISP-aware supervision as key experimental factors. Our code is available at https://github.com/mv-lab/AISP

</details>


### [113] [Efficient All-in-One Weather Restoration using Spectral Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02839)
*Paula Garrido-Mellado,Daniel Feijoo,Yuning Cui,Alvaro Garcia,Marcos V. Conde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FReSH-IR，一种轻量级全合一图像复原方法，通过在每个编码器-解码器尺度上将特征分解为高/低频分量，并用傅里叶基跳跃连接结合频谱分解与空间处理，在保持复原质量的同时比Transformer模型减少80%参数和计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣天气（雨、雾、雪）严重降低图像质量，影响人类感知和物理AI系统。现有复原方法计算开销大，难以处理高分辨率图像和多类型退化，需要更高效的轻量级全合一复原方案。

Method: 在层级编码器-解码器架构的每个尺度上显式将特征分解为高/低频分量，通过傅里叶基跳跃连接实现频谱分解与空间处理的结合，捕获互补频率信息同时不损失空间细节。

Result: 在相似复原质量下，比Transformer模型减少80%的参数和操作量，达到高效的性能-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了频谱谐波重建的有效性，为资源受限系统中的图像去雨/去雾/去雪提供了一个实用的效率-性能平衡方案，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Adverse weather conditions such as rain, haze, and snow significantly degrade image quality, posing challenges for both human perception and physical AI. Existing restoration methods require large computational budgets, struggling to process high-resolution images and handle different degradations. In this paper, we present Frequency Reconstruction via Spectral Harmonization, a novel lightweight all-in-one restoration method that explicitly decomposes feature representations into high- and low-frequency components at each scale of a hierarchical encoder-decoder architecture. By combining spectral decomposition with spatial processing through Fourier-based skip connections, FReSH-IR captures complementary frequency information without sacrificing spatial detail. Our approach achieves similar restoration quality with 80% fewer parameters and operations than transformer-based models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method offers a great efficiency-performance trade-off, highlighting its practical applications in constrained-resource systems.

</details>


### [114] [RoGe: Novel View Synthesis via End-to-End Implicit Reconstruction and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02847)
*Xiaolei Lang,Ze Kang,Zehao Huang,Naiyan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoGe是一个端到端的统一重建与生成框架，用于从稀疏视角进行新视角合成。它用前馈重建模型构建隐式场景表征，并用目标相机光线查询获得逐视角几何特征，直接注入视频扩散模型作为条件，移除了传统方法中渲染图像或显式3D表征（点图、3D高斯）作为"显式桥梁"的做法。两个模块联合训练，使生成目标直接塑造几何条件。在DL3DV上优于重建、生成及混合基线，消融实验验证了光线查询的隐式特征优于原始重建token和渲染RGB，联合训练带来额外增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视图新视角合成需要同时利用观测视图的几何基元和未观测区域的生成先验，现有混合方法通过渲染图像或显式3D表征（点图、3D高斯）桥接重建与生成，但这种显式桥接是有损且不完美的场景投影，会继承重建误差，且重建无法从生成中获得纠正信号。

Method: RoGe采用端到端统一架构：1) 前馈重建模型从稀疏输入视图构建隐式场景表征；2) 用目标相机光线查询获得逐视角几何特征；3) 将这些特征直接注入视频扩散模型作为条件（不经过任何3D中间表示）；4) 两个模块联合训练，生成损失直接作用于几何条件。

Result: 在DL3DV基准上，RoGe在图像级指标和视频级时间一致性上均优于重建型、生成型和混合型基线。消融实验证明光线查询的隐式特征优于原始重建token和渲染RGB作为条件，联合训练进一步带来增益。

Conclusion: 移除重建与生成之间的显式3D桥梁、让生成目标直接塑造几何条件，能更有效地结合稀疏视图的几何基元和生成先验，实现场景内漫游的时序连贯视频合成。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis from sparse inputs requires both geometric grounding from the observed views and generative priors of unobserved regions, motivating recent hybrid methods that combine reconstruction and generation. However, existing methods bridge the two with rendered images or explicit 3D representations such as point maps or 3D Gaussians. Generation is thus conditioned on a lossy and imperfect projection of the scene, inheriting its errors, and reconstruction receives no signal from generation to correct them. We present RoGe, an end-to-end unified reconstruction and generation framework that removes this explicit bridge. It targets roaming within a scene anchored by sparse views: given a few posed images and a camera trajectory, it synthesizes a temporally coherent video along that trajectory. From the sparse input views, RoGe builds an implicit scene representation with a feed-forward reconstruction model, and queries it with target camera rays to obtain per-view geometric features. These features are injected into a video diffusion model as conditioning, without any 3D intermediate. Both modules are trained jointly, so the generation objective directly shapes its own geometric conditioning. We conduct experiments on DL3DV, where RoGe outperforms reconstruction-based, generation-based, and hybrid baselines on image-level metrics and video-level temporal consistency. Ablations confirm that ray-queried implicit features outperform both raw reconstruction tokens and rendered RGB as conditioning, and that joint training brings further gains.

</details>


### [115] [MuyBridge: Mobile Human Center-of-Mass Estimation from Monocular Video via Sparse Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02854)
*Aidan Bradshaw,Marco Giordano,David Rode,Andreas Habersack,Elif Basokur,Annika Kruse,Markus Tilp,Michele Magno,Peter Wolf,Luca Benini,Christoph Leitner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MuyBridge是一个在手机端运行的系统，从单目视频流估计运动员的节段质心（CoM）轨迹。它将紧凑的2D姿态网络与蒸馏的单步单目深度网络结合，通过基于解剖学和物理先验的解析度量融合来锚定度量CoM，无需3D或任务特定监督。在AthletePose3D数据集上实现33-41mm垂直CoM误差和2.3-6.6% AbsRel误差，在iPhone 15上以63 FPS姿态估计速率和2.86 Hz异步深度更新运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D质心是运动、康复和临床生物力学分析中的核心量，但现有方法要么缺乏解剖约束、要么依赖过重的多视角采集基础设施，导致教练无法在单相机、真实训练场景中便捷测量公制CoM。

Method: 耦合紧凑2D姿态网络与蒸馏的单步单目深度网络，通过一个解析度量融合模块结合解剖学先验（节段参数）和物理先验（质心约束）来锚定公制CoM轨迹，不需要3D监督也不需要任务特定标注。

Result: 在AthletePose3D（跑步、田径、花样滑冰）上实现33-41mm垂直CoM误差和2.3-6.6% AbsRel误差；以63 FPS姿态估计速率运行，深度更新2.86 Hz（异步），在iPhone 15上完成实时推理；代码开源。

Conclusion: MuyBridge展示了无需多视角基础设施即可在单手机上实现具有解剖学意义的公制CoM估计，为体育和康复场景中便携可部署的生物力学分析提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: The 3D center of mass (CoM) is a primary quantity in the biomechanical analysis of sport, rehabilitation, and clinical movement, yet existing 3D pose tracking, mesh recovery, and multi-view triangulation methods either optimize 3D keypoint accuracy without anatomical constraints or carry compute and capture infrastructure too heavy to deploy where CoM tracking is most useful. As a result, the metric CoM remains difficult for coaches and movement analysts to measure from a single camera where athletes train and compete. In this work, we introduce MuyBridge, an on-device system that estimates the athlete's segmental center of mass trajectory from a single phone camera video stream. MuyBridge couples a compact 2D pose network and a distilled single-step monocular depth network through an analytic metric fusion that uses anatomical and physical priors to anchor the metric CoM, requiring no 3D or task-specific supervision. Evaluated on the athletic movements of AthletePose3D (running, track and field, and figure skating), MuyBridge achieves 33-41 mm vertical CoM error and 2.3-6.6% absolute-relative range error (AbsRel) under a one-time calibration, and produces CoM estimates at the 63 FPS pose-estimation rate using asynchronous 2.86 Hz depth updates on iPhone 15. Code is available at: https://github.com/Abradshaw1/Muybridge

</details>


### [116] [PlantC2USeg: Cross-Scale Consistent Pre-Training for Few-Shot Unified Plant Point Cloud Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02860)
*Yu Tian,Xintong Jiang,Jan Franklin Adamowski,Shiv O. Prasher,Shangpeng Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PlantC2USeg，一个面向植物点云分割（PPCS）的深度迁移学习框架，通过跨尺度一致性学习对齐多尺度特征，并采用信息受限解码策略防止重建捷径，实现少量标签下的跨物种、跨传感条件稳定泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代作物育种需要精确的器官级分析，但传统深度学习方法依赖大量密集标注数据，获取成本高。在分布偏移场景下用最少额外训练实现统一的PPCS自适应仍是挑战。

Method: PlantC2USeg框架包括：(1) 跨尺度一致性学习，显式对齐不同空间尺度特征；(2) 信息受限解码策略，防止重建捷径并促进鲁棒适应；(3) 预训练使少样本跨物种/跨传感条件泛化稳定；(4) 统一微调配合继承阈值来降低适配开销。

Result: Soybean3D全监督下语义IoU 91.91%、实例mWCov 94.62%；20个样本下分别达89.78%和90.27%；10个样本下保持最高mWCov 83.23%、IoU 83.19%。HR3D上10-shot跨烟草、番茄、高粱平均IoU 78.41%/mWCov 79.42%；22-shot转移到SYAU-Maize达IoU 92.75%/mRec 93.51%。ShapeNet Part上类别平均mIoU 85.0%展示跨农业域的泛化。

Conclusion: PlantC2USeg显著降低了分布偏移下的适配成本，实现可扩展的植物表型分析，并展示了超出农业领域的可迁移3D表示学习能力。

Abstract: Modern crop breeding demands precise organ-level analysis for trait quantification, making plant point cloud segmentation (PPCS) increasingly important. However, conventional deep learning approaches rely heavily on densely annotated datasets that are labor-intensive to acquire. Unified PPCS adaptation from distribution-shifted examples with minimal additional training remains challenging. To address this, we propose PlantC2USeg, a deep transfer learning framework featuring cross-scale consistency learning to explicitly align features across spatial scales and an information-restricted decoding strategy that prevents reconstruction shortcuts and promotes robust adaptation. The resulting pre-training enables stable few-shot generalization across species and sensing conditions, while unified fine-tuning with inherited thresholds further reduces adaptation overhead. Under full supervision on Soybean3D, PlantC2USeg achieves the highest semantic IoU and instance mWCov among compared methods, at 91.91% and 94.62%. With 20 labeled samples, it leads both metrics at 89.78% and 90.27%; with only 10 samples, it retains the highest mWCov of 83.23% while achieving 83.19% IoU. Across HR3D, 10-shot transfer to tobacco, tomato, and sorghum averages 78.41% IoU and 79.42% mWCov, while 22-shot transfer to SYAU-Maize achieves the highest IoU and mRec at 92.75% and 93.51%. Furthermore, a leading category-averaged mIoU of 85.0% on ShapeNet Part demonstrates the framework's capability to handle diverse shape variations beyond agricultural domains. These results demonstrate that PlantC2USeg reduces overall adaptation effort under distribution shifts, enabling scalable plant phenotyping and transferable 3D representation learning beyond agriculture.

</details>


### [117] [Thinking in Pictures: A Systematic Benchmark for Reasoning-driven Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02864)
*Yutong Liu,Nan Huang,Xu Cao,James M. Rehg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RIG-BENCH是一个系统评估"推理驱动图像生成"(RIG)能力的基准，包含2000个精心设计的样本，横跨基于概念、基于变换、模式与结构、基于场景四个认知要求高的领域，用于测试统一生成模型和世界模拟器从视觉输入推断潜在规则并生成逻辑严谨输出的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一生成模型和世界模拟器虽然在视觉感知与合成上表现优异，但主要依赖表面级事件对齐，缺乏高层视觉推理能力。真正的视觉生成智能需要"推理到生成"——从视觉输入推断潜在规则并通过逻辑约束的视觉输出呈现解决方案，但目前缺乏系统评估这一能力的基准。

Method: 构建RIG-BENCH评估集，涵盖四个认知需求高的评估域：基于概念、基于变换、模式与结构、基于场景。包含2000个精选样本作为压力测试数据，用于系统评估推理驱动图像生成能力，并对比多种SOTA UGM和图像/视频生成模型的差异。

Result: 对多个SOTA统一生成模型和图像/视频生成模型进行广泛评估后发现存在显著的"推理-生成鸿沟"：模型经常产生局部看似合理但全局不合逻辑的输出，说明现有模型在高层次视觉推理方面存在明显不足。

Conclusion: RIG-BENCH为诊断和引导下一代具有逻辑基础的统一生成模型与世界模拟器的发展提供了重要评估框架，揭示了现有生成模型在全局逻辑一致性推理上的关键短板。

Abstract: Recent advancements in unified generative models (UGMs) and world simulators have achieved unprecedented results in visual perception and synthesis. However, these models primarily rely on surface-level event alignment, leaving the capacity for high-level visual reasoning underexplored. True visual generative intelligence demands "Reasoning-to-Generation", an ability to infer latent rules from visual inputs and manifest solutions through precise, logically constrained visual outcomes. We introduce RIG-BENCH, a novel comprehensive benchmark that systematically evaluates Reasoning-driven Image Generation (RIG) across four cognitively demanding domains: Concept-based, Transformation-based, Pattern & Structure, and Scenario-based. Featuring 2000 curated samples, RIG-BENCH serves as a rigorous stress test for RIG. Our extensive evaluations of state-of-the-art UGMs and image/video generation models reveal a significant reasoning-generation gap, wherein models frequently produce locally plausible but globally illogical outputs. RIG-BENCH provides a vital diagnostic framework to guide the development of next-generation, logically grounded UGMs and world simulators.

</details>


### [118] [SolarWM: Open Data and Scalable Training for Long-Horizon Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02886)
*Junchao Huang,Guian Fang,Shengju Qian,Xianghao Kong,Zhuoran Zhao,Wei Huang,Yihua Du,Zixin Zhang,Justin Cui,Yuchao Gu,Yukang Chen,Xinting Hu,Tianyu He,Shaoshuai Shi,Zhuotao Tian,Xin Wang,Mike Zheng Shou,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SolarWM 是一个完全开放的基础框架，面向交互式视频世界模型，覆盖从数据准备到长时程推理的完整流程。它通过可重构的多源数据引擎和骨干网络原生适配框架，将来自10个数据集的143万个标准片段统一为帧对齐的数据契约，并基于Wan2.2、LTX-2.5和MiniMax-H3实例化了四个5B-33B规模的因果模型，支持在仅5秒序列训练后实现分钟到小时的实时交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构数据源（时间尺度、相机几何、视觉质量、运动、标注风格差异大）与异构视频骨干网络（表征和架构不同）的耦合导致朴素数据混合和逐模型定制实现产生不一致的监督信号，且难以复现和对比，亟需统一的开放式基础框架来解决这一耦合问题。

Method: 1) 可重构多源数据引擎：将143万个片段转换为统一帧对齐契约（视觉观测、度量相机几何、字幕、质量元数据、选择决策、来源信息），解耦源处理与混合构建；2) 骨干网络原生适配框架：在共享相机条件、训练和推理接口下，保留各模型原生表征和目标训练多个5B-33B模型；3) 统一三阶段训练配方：双向适配、教师强制自回归初始化、分布匹配蒸馏。

Result: 成功在Wan2.2、LTX-2.5、MiniMax-H3上实例化四个5B-33B因果世界模型，保持各自原生表征与目标；仅用5秒序列训练即可实现分钟到小时长时程rollout的实时交互能力。

Conclusion: SolarWM通过开放数据、流水线、训练配方、权重和框架，提供了一个可复现、可扩展的交互式世界模型研究基础，解决了异构数据与异构架构耦合的长期痛点。

Abstract: We introduce SolarWM, a fully open foundation for building interactive video world models from data preparation through long-horizon inference. Training across heterogeneous data sources and video backbones is challenging: datasets differ in temporal scale, camera geometry, visual quality, motion, and captioning styles, while video generators use distinct representations and architectures. Naive data mixing and model-specific implementations therefore produce inconsistent supervision and make results difficult to reproduce and compare. SolarWM addresses this coupling with a reconfigurable multi-source data engine and a backbone-native adaptation framework. The engine converts 1.43 million canonical clips from 10 datasets into a unified, frame-aligned contract covering visual observations, metric camera geometry, captions, quality metadata, selection decisions, and provenance, while decoupling source processing from mixture construction. Under shared camera-conditioning, training, and inference interfaces, we instantiate four 5B--33B models based on Wan2.2, LTX-2.5, and MiniMax-H3 while preserving their native representations and objectives. A unified three-stage recipe combines bidirectional adaptation, teacher-forced autoregressive initialization, and distribution matching distillation. The resulting causal models enable real-time interaction over rollouts ranging from minutes to hours after being trained on only 5s sequences. By releasing the resulting data, pipeline, recipes, weights, and framework, SolarWM provides a reproducible and extensible foundation for interactive world-model research.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Not All Agreement Counts as Corroboration: Provenance-Conserving Multi-View Fusion for Typed Action Admission in Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01662)
*Zekai Jin,Hanrong Zhang,Yihong Tang,Fei Hu,Zhen Dong,Yi Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACT提出将证据可数性（evidential countability）作为关系变量，用于保留来源的融合和有类型的动作准入。通过定义供应来源划分来决定可数单位，保留单位内坐标支持、仅跨单位累积，并将未满足的释放条件映射为保留、确认或回退。在31,200次评估和离线人机协作实验中验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于具身系统，预测一致性（predictive agreement）本身不足以判断证据是否值得触发动作——证据来源同样重要。反复对单一观测进行推理可以增加一致性但未增加新证据，而源局部值无法揭示输出是否具有可独立计数的来源。现有方法混淆了计算复合性与证据复合性。

Method: PACT将证据可数性形式化为约束关系变量。方法要点包括：(1) 由外部供应的来源划分（provenance partition）定义可数单位；(2) 保留每个单位内的坐标级支持（coordinatewise support），仅跨单位进行累积（accumulation）；(3) 将未满足的释放条件映射为三种动作：hold（保留）、confirm（确认）或fallback（回退）；(4) 理论上证明源局部值无法识别可数性，坐标级交（coordinatewise meet）是满足单例保真和插入非放大的最大预算，并具有粗化单调性和固定划分稳定性。

Result: 在48个场景簇的31,200次评估中，PACT达到共同的归一化风险覆盖面积（ncsAURC）0.0861。排除对抗性共识分支后，来源划分聚合相对于单例聚合将ncsAURC降低0.0557。完整来源记录上，原生分数偏好PACT，但共同后验峰值分数缩小了其与嵌套Dirichlet的差距并有利于乘积融合。随机分配来源且预测不变时，证据预算按预测迁移。离线人机协作中，8倍相机内重复使每检查点720个类型化响应不变；按相机分组的PACT在60个episode中接纳了47/57个Qwen3-VL-32B参考一致候选，且未观察到参考不一致的接纳。

Conclusion: PACT将计算复合性（computational multiplicity）与证据复合性（evidential multiplicity）分离：只有当来源允许独立累积时，一致性才构成真正的佐证（corroboration）。重复推理产生的一致性在没有可计数来源的情况下不应被视为新的证据。

Abstract: For embodied systems, predictive agreement alone does not determine whether evidence warrants action; evidential origin matters. Repeated inference over one observation can multiply agreement without adding evidence, while source-local values do not reveal whether outputs have separately countable origins. PACT treats evidence countability as a relational variable for provenance-conserving fusion and typed action admission. A supplied provenance partition defines countable units. PACT retains coordinatewise support shared within each unit, accumulates only across units, and maps unmet release conditions to hold, confirm, or fallback. Under the stated assumptions, source-local values cannot identify countability; the coordinatewise meet is the greatest budget satisfying singleton fidelity and insertion non-amplification, with coarsening monotonicity and fixed-partition stability. Across 31,200 evaluations in 48 scene clusters, PACT attains a common-support normalized risk-coverage area (ncsAURC) of 0.0861. Excluding the constructed adversarial-consensus arm, provenance-partition aggregation reduces ncsAURC by 0.0557 relative to singleton aggregation, while the corroboration contrast vanishes. On complete-source records, native scores favor PACT, but a common posterior-peak score narrows its difference from nested Dirichlet and favors product fusion. Reassigning provenance over unchanged predictions moves evidence budgets as predicted. In offline human-robot collaboration, eightfold within-camera duplication leaves 720 typed responses per checkpoint unchanged; camera-grouped PACT admits 47 of 57 Qwen3-VL-32B reference-consistent candidates with no observed reference-inconsistent admission in 60 episodes. PACT separates computational from evidential multiplicity: agreement constitutes corroboration only when provenance permits separate accumulation.

</details>


### [120] [TriSAR: Task Coordination and Collision Avoidance for Aerial Robot Teams in Disaster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01731)
*Aditya Anil Kapile,Pedro Machado,Isibor Kennedy Ihianle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TriSAR是一个五UAV协同灾害响应系统，在Gazebo地震受损城市环境中进行受控实验，采用2x2因子设计（遗传算法vs贪心任务分配 × 反应式避碰开关），通过30次随机episode评估安全性与任务效率。结果显示反应式斥力带来显著且依赖分配策略的安全效益，而GA分配仅在避碰关闭时才表现出可检测的效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多UAV灾害响应系统的任务分配与局部轨迹控制两个协调层的独立及联合贡献缺乏受控实验条件下的量化表征，需要系统性地评估各层对任务效率与操作安全性的影响。

Method: 构建五UAV的TriSAR协调系统，在基于物理引擎的Gazebo仿真（地震损坏城市环境）中运行。采用2x2因子设计：任务分配策略（GA vs 贪心适应度分配）和反应式碰撞避免（启用/禁用）。每种配置在30个随机episode中评估，场景含5个UAV和8个目标，使用Mann-Whitney检验和Welch t检验进行统计分析。

Result: 贪心分配下启用斥力彻底消除碰撞阈值违例（U=885, p=4.03x10^-12, r=0.97）；GA分配下也有显著保护效果（U=675, p=1.26x10^-5, r=0.50）。效率方面，启用斥力时GA无显著优势；禁用斥力时GA在步数、路径长度和能量上有显著优势（|g|介于0.92-1.76）。

Conclusion: 反应式斥力提供了显著且依附于分配策略的安全效益；GA任务分配的计算复杂度仅在斥力关闭时才转化为可检测的任务效率优势，说明优先级层次间的交互效应需要在仿真环境中进行精细的实验设计和统计验证。

Abstract: Multi-Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) disaster-response systems require coordinated task assignment and local trajectory control, yet the individual and combined contributions of these coordination layers to mission efficiency and operational safety remain insufficiently characterised under controlled experimental conditions. TriSAR is evaluated as a five-UAV coordination system operating in a physics-based Gazebo simulation of an earthquake-damaged urban environment. A 2 x 2 factorial design compares two task-allocation strategies (Genetic Algorithm and greedy fitness-based allocation) with reactive collision avoidance enabled or disabled. Each of the four configurations was evaluated over 30 stochastic episodes in a common scenario of five UAVs and eight targets. Under greedy allocation, enabling repulsion eliminated recorded collision-threshold violations, confirmed by a Mann-Whitney test (U = 885, p = 4.03 x 10^-12, rank-biserial r = 0.97). Under GA allocation, the same protective effect was confirmed (U = 675, p = 1.26 x 10^-5, rank-biserial r = 0.50). For mission-efficiency metrics, GA-based allocation showed no statistically detectable advantage over greedy allocation when repulsion was enabled, but a significant advantage in steps, path length, and energy when repulsion was disabled (Welch's t-tests, |g| between 0.92 and 1.76). These results show that reactive repulsion provides a substantial, allocation-dependent safety benefit, while the additional computational complexity of GA-based task allocation yields a detectable mission-efficiency benefit only when repulsion is disabled.

</details>


### [121] [Designing Versatile Samples for Learned Trajectory Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01799)
*Yaguang Li,Jiaru Zhang,Chuheng Wei,Can Cui,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种为端到端驾驶策略中的轨迹评分器设计更具信息量的训练数据集的方法。通过两个生成器分别沿横向（朝可行驶边界）和纵向（朝前车）扰动人类轨迹，构造出比基础规划器候选池更具区分度的正负样本，训练transformer评分器附加在两个冻结的生成式规划器上，在NAVSIM数据集上分别提升0.4和0.3 EPDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶策略中轨迹选择是可分离的组件，评分器可以独立训练。但现有强规划器生成的候选轨迹集中于安全模式，导致评分器在决策边界附近的监督信号不足，限制了评分器的判别能力。

Method: 设计两个轨迹扰动生成器：(1) 横向扰动——将记录的人类轨迹向可行驶边界偏移；(2) 纵向扰动——将轨迹向领航前车靠近。由此构造更富信息量的正负训练样本。将transformer评分器附加到两个冻结的生成式规划器（DiffusionDrive和MeanFuser）上，在NAVSIM navtrain数据集上训练。

Result: 在NAVSIM上取得90.1 EPDMS（DiffusionDrive）和90.4 EPDMS（MeanFuser），使用ResNet-34，分别较基准提升0.4和0.3 EPDMS。

Conclusion: 通过针对性地构造决策边界附近的扰动样本，可以显著提升轨迹评分器的训练质量，从而改善端到端自动驾驶的整体规划性能，且该方法与所用评分器架构和生成式规划器解耦，具有泛化性。

Abstract: Many current end-to-end driving policies emit a pool of candidate trajectories and select one, which makes selection a separable component: a scorer can be retrained while the planner, its backbone, and its trajectory generator all stay frozen. However, many strong planners concentrate their proposals around safe mode, providing limited supervision near decision boundaries. In this work, we design a training dataset that provides more informative supervision for the scorer. In particular, we construct two generators that perturb the logged human trajectory along the two axes a vehicle can be displaced: laterally toward the drivable boundary and longitudinally toward a leading vehicle. The designed dataset produces more informative positive and negative samples than the base planner's proposal pool. We attach a transformer-based scorer to two frozen generative planners, DiffusionDrive and MeanFuser, and train it on the NAVSIM navtrain dataset. The results of the experiments show that we achieve 90.1 EPDMS on DiffusionDrive and 90.4 EPDMS on MeanFuser when using ResNet-34, with 0.4 and 0.3 EPDMS respectively, from the designed training dataset.

</details>


### [122] [One Demonstration, Many Objects: Generalizing Manipulation via Local Contact Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01938)
*Satvik Sharma,Samrat Sahoo,Huang Huang,Fei-Fei Li Jiajun Wu,Dorsa Sadigh,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DemoMimic是一种基于动作模仿的灵巧操作策略，通过聚焦物体接触点附近的局部几何结构，设计接触中心化的奖励函数来提升sim-to-real一致性，最终实现了单一真实世界策略跨16个物体、4个任务和2种机器人手形平台迁移，成功率达71%，且相较基线具有最小的sim-to-real性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作需要大规模多指手数据，但采集困难。现有方法通过人类示教学习获得先验，但基于强化学习的sim-to-real方法常缺乏对精确接触的显式奖励激励，导致真实世界表现弱，且对未见物体泛化差。

Method: 提出DemoMimic策略，核心思想是聚焦接触点附近的物体局部几何来进行操作。设计接触中心化的奖励函数，显式鼓励精确接触，改善sim-to-real一致性，从而无需额外适配即可跨不同形状、尺度、质量、摩擦力的物体迁移。

Result: 在16个物体、4个任务、2种机器人手形平台上实现71%的真实世界成功率，与基线相比sim-to-real性能下降最小。真实世界消融实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将模仿学习与接触中心化奖励相结合，可以在真实世界中获得跨异构物体泛化的单一灵巧操作策略，证明了局部接触结构建模对sim-to-real迁移的关键作用。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation with multi-fingered robot hands promises human-level dexterity, but collecting large-scale dexterous robot hand data remains difficult. Learning from human demonstrations has emerged as a scalable alternative to robot teleoperation, providing strong priors on object interaction and contact strategies. Recent sim-to-real RL methods incorporate such priors, but often (i) omit rewards that explicitly incentivize precise contact, yielding weak real-world performance, and/or (ii) generalize poorly to unseen object instances. We propose DemoMimic (Dexterous Motion Mimic), a policy that manipulates objects by focusing on their geometry local to the contact points. Its contact-centric rewards encourage precise contact and improve sim-to-real consistency, yielding a single real-world policy that transfers across objects of varying shape, scale, mass, and friction wherever local contact structure is preserved. Real-world ablations show that DemoMimic achieves 71% success across 16 objects, four tasks, and two robot-hand embodiments, with the smallest sim-to-real drop compared to baselines.

</details>


### [123] [MACAW: Reliable And Efficient Surgical Debridement Using Monocular Adaptive Compact Attention Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01961)
*Ziyang Chen,Shutong Jin,Preethi Satish,Sareena Mann,Cael Magner,Danyal Fer,Omid Mohareri,Gary Guthart,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一套用于手术清创的增强灵巧度系统，通过视觉伺服在图像平面内对齐线缆驱动夹爪，并提出MACAW（单目自适应紧凑注意力窗口）实现深度控制。在dVRK机器人上100次物理试验中，相机帧伺服将平均夹爪位置偏移从37像素降至5像素以内（4步优化，平均0.39秒）。MACAW显著优于程序和学习的VLA基线，单臂成功率93%（11秒/碎片，304片/小时）；双臂设置保持92%成功率（7秒/碎片，473片/小时）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强外科医生的灵巧度可将其从繁琐子任务中解放出来。清创手术因空间感知不精确和线缆驱动造成的误差而极具挑战性，需要自动化系统来提升手术效率与准确性。

Method: 系统分为两层：(1) 视觉伺服：通过相机帧内对齐线缆驱动夹爪与目标位置，最小化图像平面偏移；(2) MACAW（Monocular Adaptive Compact Attention Windows）：一种新颖的单目深度控制方法，使用自适应紧凑注意力窗口来估计深度并引导夹爪接近目标。系统在单臂和双臂两种清笨场景下进行了验证。

Result: 相机帧伺服在100次物理试验中平均约0.39s内将位置偏移从37像素降至5像素以内。MACAW成功率93%（11秒/碎片，304片/小时），显著优于程序和VLA基线。双臂扩展保持92%成功率，平均7秒/碎片，吞吐量提升至473片/小时。

Conclusion: 该增强灵巧度清创系统结合视觉伺服与MACAW深度控制，在真实机器人上实现了高成功率和高吞吐量的手术清创，验证了其在手术自动化中的有效性，双臂配置进一步提升了效率。

Abstract: Augmenting the dexterity of human surgeons has the potential to free them from tedious subtasks. We consider debridement (removal of diseased or dead tissue fragments), which is challenging due to imprecision in spatial perception and cable actuation. We develop an augmented dexterity system for surgical debridement that uses visual servoing to align the cable-driven gripper with the target position in the image plane, and then introduces a novel approach to depth control, MACAW: Monocular Adaptive Compact Attention Windows. Across 100 physical trials using the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) robot, camera-frame servoing reduced average gripper position offset from 37 to fewer than 5 pixels within 4 optimization steps, taking an average of only 0.39s. MACAW significantly outperforms procedural and learned VLA baselines, achieving a 93% success rate at 11 seconds per fragment, yielding a throughput of 304 fragments per hour. Extending MACAW to a bimanual debridement setup maintains a 92% success rate at an average of 7 seconds per fragment, increasing the throughput to 473 fragments per hour.

</details>


### [124] [Design and Validation of a Lightweight, Low-Profile Powered Knee Prosthesis with Quasi-Direct Drive Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02003)
*Ross J. Cortino,Ryan Posh,Emily G. Keller,Robert D. Gregg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一款新型轻量化（2.6 kg）低轮廓（24.5 cm）准直驱（QDD）膝关节假肢，通过优化18:1两级传动及有限元热/结构分析，实现145 Nm峰值扭矩、1 Nm低反向驱动扭矩，并在三名不同K-level的经股截肢者上验证了仿生运动学和峰值伸膝力矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统膝关节假肢无法提供可控正功，而QDD相较传统高减速比执行器具有更好的扭矩控制、反向驱动性和低噪声优势，但此前的QDD原型过于笨重庞大，难以商业化。作者旨在解决QDD假肢的质量与体积瓶颈。

Method: 对18:1两级传动系统进行优化设计，结合热分析和结构有限元分析以减轻质量，同时保证输出性能。通过台架测试验证高输出扭矩、低反向驱动扭矩及精确的位置/扭矩控制，并在三名不同K-level经股截肢者上评估行走和坐站切换时的运动学与力矩表现。

Result: 实现了2.6 kg质量和24.5 cm构建高度，145 Nm峰值扭矩，1 Nm反向驱动扭矩；在水平行走和坐站切换中，受试者的峰值伸膝力矩均在能-bodied参考值一个标准差以内，展示了仿生运动学性能，同时在质量、高度、峰值扭矩和噪声方面达到或超过领先商用动力膝关节。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了新兴QDD假肢在临床上的可行性，其轻量化与高性能的结合预示着改进用户动态活动能力的潜力。

Abstract: Fully-powered knee prostheses, unlike traditional passive knees, can perform controlled positive work, reducing the need for compensatory behaviors by users during energy-intensive activities. While quasi-direct drive (QDD) actuators provide superior torque control, backdrivability, and acoustic noise properties compared to traditional highly-geared actuators, prior QDD prototypes have been too heavy and bulky for commercial translation. In this work, we present the design and validation of a new lightweight (2.6 kg) and low-profile (24.5 cm tip-to-tip build height) QDD knee prosthesis. By optimizing an 18 to 1 two-stage transmission alongside thermal and structural finite-element analyses, we significantly reduce device mass while enabling a peak torque of 145 Nm. Through benchtop tests, we validate the device's high output torque, low backdrive torque (1 Nm), and its precision position and torque control capabilities. We also demonstrate biomimetic kinematics and peak knee extension torques (within one standard deviation of able-bodied references) during both level-ground walking and sit-stand transitions performed by three participants with transfemoral amputation and varying K-levels. By meeting or improving upon the mass, build height, peak torque, and acoustic noise of a leading commercial powered knee, this work establishes the clinical viability of emerging QDD prostheses that promise improved dynamic performance for their users.

</details>


### [125] [Real-Time Dynamics-Based Torque-Sampling MPPI for Compliant and Force Aware Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02020)
*Euncheol Im,Taehyun Kim,Yonghwan Oh,Myotaeg Lim,Yisoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于MPPI的任务空间控制框架，在实时MPC公式中显式求解刚体动力学并施加安全约束，实现机器人操作的精确运动和力控制及柔顺行为。通过扭矩采样架构利用GPU并行化，在7自由度机械臂上实际验证，求解器更新率超过166Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化环境中执行安全的物理交互（如柔顺和力控）对机器人操作至关重要，但常规MPC方法难以实时处理非线性刚体动力学，需要一种既能高效求解非线性问题又能保证安全约束的实时控制框架。

Method: 提出MPPI任务空间控制框架：1) 在实时MPC公式中显式求解刚体动力学；2) 通过MPPI有效处理传统MPC难以实时求解的非线性动力学；3) 开发扭矩采样架构以利用GPU并行化，实现高效的柔顺和力感知行为；4) 施加安全约束确保物理交互的安全性。

Result: 所提框架在0.18s预测时域内实现超过166Hz的求解器更新率，并在7自由度机械臂上通过真实世界实验验证了性能，展示了准确的运动和力控制及柔顺行为。

Conclusion: MPPI结合GPU并行化的任务空间控制框架可实时求解非线性刚体动力学并保证安全约束，为实现非结构化环境中的安全柔顺物理交互提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This study proposes a novel Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI)-based task-space control framework. The proposed framework explicitly solves rigid-body dynamics within a real-time MPC formulation and enforces safety constraints, enabling accurate motion and force control that yields compliant behaviors for safe and effective physical interaction of robotic manipulators in unstructured environments. By leveraging MPPI, the proposed framework efficiently handles nonlinear dynamics that are difficult to solve with conventional MPC approaches in real-time. Furthermore, we develop a torque-sampling-based control architecture that enables efficient exploitation of GPU-based parallelization, resulting in effective compliant and force-aware behaviors. As a result, the proposed framework achieves a solver update rate of over 166 Hz with a 0.18 s prediction horizon, and its performance is validated through real-world experiments on a 7-DoF manipulator.

</details>


### [126] [Modeling What Changes: Sparse, Residual World Models for Object-Centric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02046)
*Param Thakkar,Parsika Paresh Shah,Manisha Sushant Gote*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出稀疏/残差世界模型：仅对被门控标记的物体进行残差delta预测，而非重预测整个场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整体（monolithic）世界模型每步重预测整个下一状态，浪费容量在静态背景上并向其注入误差；作者质疑显式建模变化是否更适合物理预测与控制。

Method: 每物体变化门控（change gate）+ 残差delta头，仅扰动门控标记的物体；在MuJoCo桌面推物任务上从3到8个物体进行缩放对比稠密MLP基线。

Result: 下一状态位姿预测精度提高2.5-4.6倍且参数减少8.6-11.1倍；变化检测F1达0.80-0.87（基线退化）；零重训练跨物体数量迁移（F1保留99.4%）；四分之一数据达到约90%全量精度；自回归rollout误差累积大幅减少。

Conclusion: 建模"什么在变化"而非重预测整个世界，是面向物体中心物理AI的简单有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Monolithic world models predict the entire next state at every step, spending capacity re-predicting the static majority of a scene and injecting error into it. We ask whether explicitly modeling change (a per-object change gate plus a residual delta head that perturbs only the objects the gate flags) is a more effective and interpretable bias for physical prediction and control. On a MuJoCo tabletop pushing benchmark scaling from 3 to 8 objects, the sparse/residual model predicts next-state poses 2.5 to 4.6 times more accurately than a dense multilayer perceptron at 8.6 to 11.1 times fewer parameters, sustains change-detection F1 of 0.80 to 0.87 where the dense baseline is degenerate, transfers across object counts with zero retraining (99.4 percent F1 retention), and reaches about 90 percent of its full-data accuracy with a quarter of the data. In autoregressive rollout it compounds far less error, hugging the no-motion floor while the dense model drifts. Finally, inside a sampling-based planner, prediction-only models fail (though a true-simulator oracle solves the task with the identical planner, confirming the planner is sound), but once featurized and trained for the states a planner visits, the sparse model begins to plan (0.23 plus or minus 0.06 success over three seeds) while the dense monolith stays at zero at every seed. Modeling what changes, rather than re-predicting the whole world, is a simple, effective bias for object-centric physical AI; code, data generators, and all checkpoints will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [127] [Koopman-Based Robust Model Predictive Control for Nonlinear Systems with Stochastic Intermittent Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02079)
*Guanhua Liu,Tong Wu,Lixian Zhang,Weifeng Du,Minghao Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Koopman的随机模型预测控制（MPC）框架，针对间歇性状态测量下的约束非线性系统，采用双模态马尔可夫链建模测量中断与重置过程，结合Lipschitz约束的深度Koopman模型进行潜在线性预测，并通过概率截断的软约束实现计算高效的在线优化，在可验证条件下建立了闭环误差的均方最终有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间歇性状态测量（传感器丢包、反馈中断）对约束非线性系统的MPC构成根本性挑战：预测不确定性在反馈中断期间增长，测量触发的状态重置破坏标称状态传播，可能危及闭环稳定性和递归可行性。现有方法缺乏统一的处理框架。

Method: 1) 采用Lipschitz约束的深度Koopman模型构建线性潜在预测器；2) 将间歇测量建模为双模态离散时间马尔可夫链，形成统一的Markov跳变误差模型；3) 推导预测误差的均方最终有界性和显式均匀二阶矩界；4) 构造无分布概率误差半径用于截断丢包依赖的约束收紧；5) 采用精确罚软约束机制处理重置引起的跳变和长时间丢包。

Result: 理论结果表明在终端兼容性和有界扰动条件下，闭环调节误差满足递归可行性和均方最终有界性。数值仿真在视觉伺服跟踪任务上验证了理论结果，展示了在随机测量不可用情况下仍能有效跟踪。

Conclusion: 该框架为间歇测量条件下的安全关键控制系统提供了理论上严谨、计算上可行的MPC方案，兼顾了Koopman方法的高效性和随机框架的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Intermittent state measurements pose fundamental challenges to model predictive control of constrained nonlinear systems because prediction uncertainty grows during feedback outages and measurement-triggered resets disrupt nominal state propagation, potentially compromising closed-loop stability and recursive feasibility. This paper develops a Koopman-based stochastic MPC framework with probabilistically truncated soft constraints. Specifically, a Lipschitz-constrained deep Koopman model provides a linear latent predictor, enabling computationally efficient online optimization. The intermittent measurement process is modeled as a two-mode discrete-time Markov chain, yielding a unified Markov jump error model for open-loop propagation and measurement-triggered resets. Under numerically verifiable sufficient conditions, the prediction error is shown to be mean-square ultimately bounded, and an explicit uniform second-moment bound is obtained. A distribution-free probabilistic error radius is then constructed for a prescribed confidence level and used to truncate dropout-dependent constraint tightening. An exact-penalty soft-constraint mechanism accommodates reset-induced jumps and prolonged dropouts. Under the stated terminal compatibility and bounded-disturbance conditions, recursive feasibility and mean-square ultimate boundedness of the closed-loop regulation error are established. Numerical simulations on a visual-servoing tracking task corroborate these theoretical results and demonstrate effective tracking under stochastic measurement unavailability.

</details>


### [128] [Unified Motion Retargeting for Humanoids with Learned Point Cloud Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02134)
*Hanyang Cao,Yuetong Fang,Taesoo Kwon,Runyi Yu,Ji Ma,Jing Tan,Yangchen Zhou,Baoze Du,Yi Gu,Yukang Gao,Ruoli Dai,Lei Han,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UMR是一种统一运动重定向框架，通过学习密集点云对应关系，无需手动设计的人-机器人映射，即可将异构人体动作数据重定向到多种人形机器人上，实现表面级姿态对齐和交互接触的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作重定向方法依赖手工设计的稀疏关键点或身体部位对应关系，可扩展性差，难以跨动作来源和机器人形态泛化，对精细姿态和交互接触只能提供稀疏引导。

Method: 将外部点云作为人体动作与人形机器人之间的统一接口，学习密集点云对应关系以解耦源骨骼语义和机器人拓扑；利用学到的密集对应为约束点云匹配优化提供细粒度几何锚点，实现表面级姿态对齐和交互接触的直接迁移。

Result: 实验表明UMR能统一异构动作来源、机器人形态和下游场景（从运动到交互）的重定向，在运动保真度和合理性上优于现有最优方法。

Conclusion: UMR为人形机器人学习提供了一种可扩展的基础方案，能将大规模人体动作参考高效转化为机器人可用的训练数据。

Abstract: Humanoid learning increasingly relies on transforming vast and diverse human motion data into high-quality robot reference trajectories. However, retargeting human motion to humanoid robots is challenging due to substantial differences in morphology, degrees of freedom, joint ranges, and kinematic constraints between humans and robots. Existing retargeting methods typically address these differences by defining human-robot correspondence through hand-crafted sparse keypoints or body-part pairs. As a result, retargeting quality depends heavily on manual semantic design, limiting scalability across motion sources and robot morphologies and providing only sparse guidance for reproducing detailed poses and interactions. In this paper, we present Unified Motion Retargeting (UMR), a framework that learns dense point cloud correspondence without requiring manually designed human-robot mappings. By treating exterior point clouds as a unified interface between human motion and humanoid robots, UMR decouples retargeting from source-specific skeletal semantics and robot-specific topology. The learned dense correspondence provides fine-grained geometric anchors for constrained point cloud matching optimization, enabling surface-level pose alignment and direct transfer of interaction contacts. Experiments demonstrate that UMR unifies retargeting across heterogeneous motion sources, robot embodiments, and downstream scenarios ranging from locomotion to interaction, while achieving higher motion fidelity and plausibility than state-of-the-art methods. UMR therefore provides a scalable foundation for transforming large-scale human motion references into robot-ready training data.

</details>


### [129] [Towards Effective Physical Reservoir Computing with a Pneumatic Soft Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02157)
*Jeevan Hebbal Manjunath,Jun Wang,Suyi Li,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过气动软体手臂实验系统研究了物理储层计算（PRC）中储层设计规则，考察了气囊互连拓扑、机器人刚度和传感器数量对弯曲角度估计性能的影响，提出了三条设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储层计算（PRC）利用物理动力学系统作为计算资源进行状态估计和控制，但缺乏系统的设计规则研究，导致难以有效设计物理储层。

Method: 使用带五气囊传感柱的气动软体臂，在36组匹配试验中（覆盖波形、基线压力和驱动范围变量），以同一时间的弯曲角度估计为基准（使用0.2秒压力历史和固定岭估计器），系统评估互连拓扑、刚度和传感器数量的影响。

Result: 提出三条设计准则：1）独立密封气囊比共享流形保留更丰富的可观测状态；2）提高传感柱基线压力使气囊响应更冗余，在耦合拓扑中增加估计误差最明显；3）密封拓扑中两个策略放置的传感器即可获得大部分收益，三个可捕获几乎所有收益，额外传感器价值很小。表明拓扑、刚度和传感器数量应协同设计。

Conclusion: 拓扑、刚度、传感器数量应协同设计以实现软体机器人状态的准确PRC估计；仅靠更强的激励无法恢复差设计已消除的多样性。

Abstract: Physical reservoir computing (PRC) refers to the use of a physical dynamical system as a computational resource for tasks such as state estimation and control, but there has been a lack of formal study of design rules towards more effective design of such physical reservoirs. Using a pneumatic soft arm with a five-pouch sensing column, this work studies how the pouch interconnection topology, robot stiffness, and the number of instrumented sensors affect bending-angle estimation performance. Across 36 matched trials spanning waveform, baseline pressure of the sensing column, and actuation range, all designs are evaluated under the same-time bending-angle estimation benchmark using 0.2 s of pressure history and a fixed ridge estimator. Our analysis of the experimental results leads to three design guidelines. First, independently sealed pouches preserve a much richer observable state than a shared manifold. Second, increasing the baseline pressure of the sensing column makes the pouch responses more redundant and increases estimation error most strongly in the coupled topology. Third, in the sealed topology, two strategically placed sensors already recover most of the attainable benefit, three capture essentially all of it, and additional sensors provide little or no additional value. In summary, the results suggest that topology, stiffness, and number of instrumented sensors should be co-designed for accurate PRC of soft robot states; stronger excitation alone cannot recover the diversity that poor design choices have already removed.

</details>


### [130] [Hardware-Accelerated Instance Segmentation for Resource-Constrained Space Robotics with Criticality Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02219)
*Siddhant Shete,Hilmi Dogu Kücüker,Udo Frese,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向资源受限月球机器人的部署型实例分割框架，联合解决量化校准与辐射故障暴露问题。提出激活方差信息采样(AVIS)无标签校准策略，在DPU上部署YOLO分割模型并引入软件级关键性分析。在月球微车上恢复69.8%的量化精度损失，推理延迟309ms、功耗5.7W，目标缓解降低31.7%全局关键性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主月球任务需在极端低光、有限算力与辐射故障三重约束下实现实时感知，现有工作未能联合解决量化校准与系统级故障暴露问题。

Method: 1) 提出AVIS激活方差信息采样作为无标签校准策略；2) 在DPU上部署YOLO分割模型并进行架构修改以减少CPU回退路径、支持静态编译执行与有界延迟；3) 提出软件级关键性分析以估计故障暴露并指导辐射约束下的缓解策略。

Result: AVIS配合偏置校正恢复69.8%的量化精度损失；推理延迟309ms、功耗5.7W；目标缓解策略降低31.7%的全局关键性。在月球微车平台上验证。

Conclusion: 展示了在空间部署约束下集成量化校准、硬件部署与故障缓解的可靠AI感知框架蓝图。

Abstract: Autonomous lunar missions require real-time per- ception under three coupled constraints: extreme low-light conditions, limited onboard compute, and radiation-induced hardware faults that can silently corrupt inference. We present a deployment-oriented instance segmentation framework for resource-constrained lunar robotics that jointly addresses quan- tization calibration and system-level fault exposure under strict compute constraints. First, we introduce Activation Variance Informative Sampling (AVIS), a label-free calibration strategy that deterministically selects calibration samples based on activation variance statistics. Second, we deploy a YOLO-based segmentation model on a Deep Learning Processor Unit (DPU) with architectural modifications that reduce CPU fallback paths and enable statically compiled execution with bounded latency in low-lighting conditions. We further introduce a software-level criticality analysis to estimate fault exposure and guide mitigation under radiation-constrained operation. On a lunar micro-rover platform, AVIS with bias correction recovers 69.8% of quantization-induced accuracy loss while achieving 309 ms inference latency and 5.7 W power consumption. Targeted mitigation reduces global criticality by 31.7%. The results demonstrate an integrated approach and a blueprint for a reliable and safe AI perception framework under space deployment constraints.

</details>


### [131] [FOCUS: Foot Observation Confidence for Robust Humanoid Proprioceptive Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02222)
*Kaixin Feng,Angsong Li,Shaopeng Zhang,Enyu Li,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,You Li,Haiyu Lan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FOCUS是一种基于未标注仿真数据训练的连续FK可靠性权重预测方法，用于四足机器人里程计估计。它不再使用二值接触判断，而是通过学习连续权重来平滑融合IMU传播速度和FK观测，在EKF框架内自适应调整观测协方差，显著减少了长轨迹下的漂移和绝对轨迹误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的接触辅助里程计估计依赖二值接触判断来决定是否信任整只脚的FK测量。然而接触并不代表FK可靠——动态运动常伴随部分支撑、脚趾拖拽和脚滑，二值接触判断会在长轨迹上积累显著漂移。需要一种更能刻画FK观测真实可靠性的连续置信度方法。

Method: 提出FOCUS网络，从IMU和关节运动学信号中预测每只脚连续的FK可靠性权重，而非二值接触。通过FK加权速度一致性损失和轻量级仿真器接触正则化，在自动生成的仿真信号上训练，无需人工标注连续FK可靠性标签。预测权重用于将FK速度观测与IMU传播的机体速度进行混合，并自适应调整EKF观测协方差，实现无需硬接触切换的平滑可靠性感知融合。

Result: 在仿真行走任务中绝对轨迹误差(ATE)降低83.7%；在19段真实行走数据中ATE降低70.8%；在四种真实动态运动数据中平均ATE降低42.7%，同时保持了动态运动的尺度与频谱能量保真度。

Conclusion: FOCUS通过连续FK可靠性估计替代二值接触判断，有效地缓解了部分支撑、拖拽和滑脚导致的里程计漂移问题。该方法部署仅依赖IMU和关节运动学测量，不依赖扭矩传感，适用于实际硬件平台。

Abstract: Foot forward kinematics (FK) is widely used to improve proprioceptive legged odometry by providing reliable velocity constraints during foot support. Existing contact-aided estimators generally rely on binary contact decisions to determine whether the FK measurements of an entire foot should be trusted. However, contact does not necessarily imply FK reliability. Dynamic locomotion often involves partial support, toe dragging, and foot slip, causing binary contact decisions to accumulate significant drift over long trajectories. To address this limitation, we propose FOCUS (Foot Observation Confidence from Unannotated Simulation), which predicts a continuous FK reliability weight for each foot instead of estimating binary foot contact. Rather than replacing the model-based estimator, the predicted reliability weights are used to blend FK velocity observations with IMU-propagated body velocity and to adapt the observation covariance of an extended Kalman filter (EKF), enabling smooth reliability-aware fusion without hard contact switching. The network is trained from automatically generated simulation signals using an FK-weighted velocity consistency loss with lightweight simulator-contact regularization, without manually annotated continuous FK-reliability labels. The deployed model relies only on IMU and joint kinematic measurements, making it suitable for hardware platforms with unreliable torque sensing. Experiments demonstrate that FOCUS reduces absolute trajectory error (ATE) by 83.7% on simulated walking episodes, preserves simulated dynamic-motion fidelity in motion scale and spectral energy, reduces ATE by 70.8% across 19 real walking segments, and reduces mean ATE by 42.7% across four real dynamic-motion routines.

</details>


### [132] [DiffuSearch: How Hybrid Trajectory Planning Benefits from Aligned Objectives in Diffusion and Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02252)
*Steffen Hagedorn,Aron Distelzweig,Alexandru P. Condurache*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffuSearch提出一种基于统一驾驶目标（碰撞避免、可行驶区域合规、舒适性、进度）的混合规划器，采用两阶段架构：引导扩散模型生成场景一致的联合轨迹预测（以驾驶目标作为可微引导函数），随后用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）在离散动作空间中做显式局部精化（以相同目标为奖励函数）。在nuPlan和interPlan基准上实现强性能乃至SOTA，大幅减少碰撞并提升舒适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶轨迹规划的混合架构通常由目标各异的模块拼凑而成，缺乏统一原则，导致初始轨迹与精化轨迹不一致，产生次优行为。

Method: 两阶段架构：(1) 引导扩散模型生成场景一致、联合轨迹预测，用统一驾驶目标作为可微引导函数隐式指导去噪过程；(2) MCTS在离散动作空间中进行显式局部精化，使用相同驾驶目标作为奖励函数。两个阶段共享同一组目标函数。

Result: 在nuPlan和interPlan reactive闭环基准上达到强性能且常为SOTA，显著减少碰撞、提升舒适性，尤其在复杂交互场景。消融显示MCTS精化是性能提升主因，目标共享带来额外的一致改进。

Conclusion: 通过将隐式生成（扩散引导）与显式搜索（MCTS）统一在同一套驾驶目标下，可以兼顾扩散的场景一致性优势与MCTS的可解释、约束感知精化能力，为混合规划提供了统一原理框架。

Abstract: In trajectory planning for autonomous driving, hybrid planning architectures are often realized as a collection of disparate modules, each with its own objectives. This lack of a unifying principle can lead to inconsistencies between the initial and refined trajectory, resulting in suboptimal behavior. We address this by introducing DiffuSearch, a novel hybrid planner that uses a unified set of objectives across generation and refinement. Our model encourages all components to follow the same shared driving goals: collision avoidance, drivable area compliance, comfort, and progress. DiffuSearch employs a two-stage architecture. First, a guided diffusion model generates a scene-consistent, joint trajectory prediction, using our driving objectives as differentiable guidance functions to implicitly steer the denoising process. Second, a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) in a discretized action space performs an explicit, local refinement of this proposal, leveraging the same driving objectives as its reward function. This synergistic design leverages the diffusion model's strength in finding scene-consistent solutions combined with the explainable, constraint-aware refinement of MCTS. Experiments on nuPlan and interPlan reactive closed-loop benchmarks demonstrate that DiffuSearch achieves strong and often state-of-the-art performance, substantially reducing collisions and improving comfort, particularly in complex, interactive scenarios. Our ablation studies indicate that MCTS refinement is the main mechanism behind the gains, while sharing objectives between implicit guidance and explicit search provides further consistent improvements.

</details>


### [133] [CrashDiffuser: VLM-Guided Collision Intent Reasoning for Fine-Grained Safety-Critical Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02270)
*Shucheng Zhang,Yuang Zhang,Bingzhang Wang,Muhammad Monjurul Karim,Kehua Chen,Yinhai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CrashDiffuser，一种闭环VLM引导的扩散框架，用于细粒度安全关键场景生成，要求在指定车辆接触区域（车头、车尾或侧面）发生碰撞，而非仅生成任意碰撞。系统通过分层碰撞意图接口解耦语义推理与轨迹合成，实现50.33%的单次目标碰撞率和67.98%的三次尝试成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全关键场景生成器主要关注诱导碰撞，缺乏对目标车辆接触位置的精细控制。然而实际自动驾驶评测中，碰撞发生的具体区域（前/后/侧）对于验证特定感知和决策能力至关重要，因此需要支持指定接触区域的细粒度场景生成。

Method: CrashDiffuser采用VLM引导的扩散框架，在初始化时由VLM提取可复用的场景级上下文；在每个重规划步骤中，VLM预测结构化的动作元组（速度变化、转向行为和碰撞阶段），该意图作为分层碰撞接口条件输入扩散模型生成对抗轨迹。通过碰撞引导采样、候选选择和短时域重规划，使生成过程适应目标车辆动态行为。

Result: 在WOMD衍生的闭环场景中，CrashDiffuser单次尝试目标碰撞率达50.33%，三次尝试达67.98%，接触区域控制成功率为40.05%，轨迹自然度具有竞争力。组件消融实验验证了各设计模块的有效性。

Conclusion: CrashDiffuser证明了通过语义-运动解耦（VLM语义推理配合扩散模型连续轨迹合成）可以有效实现指定接触区域的细粒度安全关键场景生成，为自动驾驶场景生成提供了新的框架范式，但其成功率仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Generating safety-critical scenarios is essential for evaluating autonomous driving systems. However, existing generators primarily focus on inducing collisions and offer limited control over where contact occurs on the target vehicle. In this paper, we study fine-grained safety-critical scenario generation, where success requires both a target collision and a specified head, rear, or side contact region. We propose CrashDiffuser, a closed-loop VLM-guided diffusion framework that decouples semantic collision reasoning from continuous trajectory synthesis through a hierarchical collision-intent interface derived from the requested target contact region. At initialization, the VLM extracts reusable scene-level context; at each replanning step, it predicts a structured action tuple describing speed change, turning behavior, and collision stage. This intent conditions a diffusion model to generate executable adversarial trajectories, while collision-guided sampling, candidate selection, and short-horizon replanning adapt generation to the target vehicle's evolving behavior. On WOMD-derived closed-loop scenarios, CrashDiffuser achieves a target-collision rate of 50.33% in a single attempt and 67.98% after three attempts, together with a contact-region control success rate of 40.05% and competitive trajectory naturalness. Component ablations further support the proposed design.

</details>


### [134] [Contact-Constrained Lower-Limb Joint-Offset Calibration for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02306)
*Kaixiang Lu,Haiyu Lan,Chunxiao Qiao,You Li,Chengyuan Luo,Enyu Li,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自包含的人形机器人下肢关节编码器偏移校准框架，仅利用关节编码器和骨盆IMU在静态双足支撑状态下实现校准，无需外部动捕系统或靶标。通过最小化前向运动学中足间变换的位姿相关离散度，构建12维偏移向量的非线性最小二乘问题，并结合Hessian特征结构与先验约束解耦弱可观测通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人下肢关节编码器偏移的精确校准对运动学一致性至关重要，但现有校准方法依赖外部动作捕捉系统或标记物，部署成本高、适用场景受限，亟需自包含且实用的校准方案。

Method: 利用双足固定时足间变换恒定的约束，最小化其位姿相关离散度构建12维偏移向量的非线性最小二乘问题；通过Hessian特征结构分析识别平行俯仰轴导致的旋转耦合，采用方向残差、髋部滚转/偏航激励及站姿膝盖先验来逐层解析弱可观测通道。

Result: 在A3和A2机器人上，足部高度RMS残差分别从4.26降至2.20mm和从8.03降至1.43mm；LiDAR-惯性独立参考验证了俯仰耦合通道，注入偏移后垂向漂移显著改善。

Conclusion: 少量静态双足站立姿态即可为激励充分的方向提供接触一致性修正，但弱俯仰链中的个别偏移仍依赖于先验，存在固有的观测性限制。

Abstract: Accurate joint encoder offsets are essential for kinematic consistency in humanoid lower limbs, yet existing calibration methods typically require external motion-capture systems or fiducial targets. We present a self-contained calibration framework exploiting only onboard joint encoders and a pelvis-mounted IMU during static double-support contact. The inter-foot transform from forward kinematics must stay constant when both feet are fixed; minimizing its posture-dependent dispersion yields a nonlinear least-squares problem over the 12-dimensional offset vector. A Hessian eigenstructure analysis shows that parallel pitch axes induce a rotational coupling. Orientation residuals then observe only the pitch-offset sum, while translation and posture diversity set the remaining numerical observability. For the A3 pitch-to-roll-to-yaw ordering, hip-roll and hip-yaw excitation reduce hip-pitch coupling. A standing-posture knee prior then anchors the remaining weak pitch-chain decomposition. Simulation and real-machine injection tests show consistent recovery, and on held-out recordings calibration reduces foot-height RMS residuals from 4.26 to 2.20 mm on A3 and from 8.03 to 1.43 mm on A2. An independent LiDAR-inertial reference checks the pitch-coupled channel. Removing an injected pitch offset moves the leg-odometry vertical drift back toward the LiDAR trajectory. A few static double-support stances thus provide contact-consistent corrections for well-excited directions. Individual offsets in the weak pitch chain remain prior-dependent.

</details>


### [135] [From Multi-Fisheye Sensing to Panoramic Perception: A Parallax-Aware Onboard Platform for Ultra-Low-Altitude UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02319)
*Dun Dai,Ze Lu,Cheng He,Yaowen Wang,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向超低空无人机(UAV)的视差感知全景感知平台。该平台将四个同步鱼眼相机流通过Jetson Orin NX嵌入式设备转换为开放的1280x640等距柱状全景(ERP)接口。其拼接管线支持按重叠区域选择投影深度，并包含两种配置：精度模式（内容自适应接缝搜索+验证门控残差网格）与部署模式（边际门控逐接缝更新以支持传感器速率）。在18个野外序列超5万组四视图上验证：精度模式将远场P90特征错位降低41.6%，部署模式在保留场地上几何误差最低；20Hz回放下维持19.99fps、13.29W功耗；八扇区ERP采样白天视觉位置识别Recall@5达90.8%。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低空无人机在近建筑物、植被及其他障碍物飞行时需要周围环境感知，现有方案缺乏嵌入式、视差感知的全景拼接受限平台，导致下游视觉任务在近距离场景中因视差错位而失效。

Method: 构建碳纤维机架整合四鱼眼相机、Jetson Orin NX、飞控和GNSS；拼接管线按重叠区域逐区选择投影深度，结合受控接缝与光度融合。精度模式引入内容自适应接缝搜索与验证门控残差网格优化几何配准；部署模式采用边际门控逐接缝(Per-seam)更新以维持传感器速率推理。下游通过八扇区ERP采样实现视觉位置识别。

Result: 精度模式相比固定深度在远场P90特征错位上降低41.6%；部署模式在保留测试场地最低聚合几何误差；20Hz回放下19.99fps、13.29W功耗；日间视觉位置识别Recall@5达90.8%；全系统硬件+软件已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了集视差感知拼接、嵌入式实时执行与可复用下游视觉接口于一体的机载全景感知架构，为超低空无人机近障碍环境感知提供了完整且开源的解决方案。

Abstract: Ultra-low-altitude unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) require surround vision near buildings, vegetation, and other obstacles. We present a parallax-aware onboard platform that converts four synchronized fisheye streams into an open 1280x640 equirectangular panorama (ERP) interface. A purpose-built carbon-fiber airframe integrates the cameras, NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX, a flight controller, and a global navigation satellite system (GNSS) receiver. The formation pipeline selects projection depth per overlap and combines controlled seams and photometric fusion. Its accuracy profile adds content-adaptive seam search and a validation-gated residual mesh, whereas its deployed profile retains margin-gated Per-seam updates for sensor-rate operation. Evaluation uses more than 50,000 four-view groups from 18 field sequences. Relative to Fixed Depth, the accuracy profile reduces far-field P90 feature misalignment by 41.6%; the deployed Per-seam profile achieves the lowest aggregate geometric errors across held-out sites. Under a paced 20 Hz replay, the deployed profile sustains 19.99 frames/s at 13.29W mean module-input power. Eight-sector ERP sampling reaches 90.8% mean daytime visual-place-recognition Recall@5. Together, these results validate an integrated onboard panoramic-perception architecture that unifies parallax-aware formation, sensor-rate embedded execution, and reusable downstream vision interfaces for ultra-low-altitude UAVs. The project has been open-sourced at https://github.com/DUNDAI1998/parallax-aware-uav-panorama.

</details>


### [136] [Humanoid Safe Stop via Learned Stoppability Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02358)
*Junfeng Long,Pieter Abbeel,Koushil Sreenath,Roberto Horowitz,Guanya Shi,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO
